local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

-- // CONFIGURATION //
local UI_CONFIG = {
    MainColor = Color3.fromRGB(15, 25, 15), -- Dark Green BG
    StrokeColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100), -- Toxic Green Outline
    TextColor = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 150),
    SubTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180),
    AccentColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 80),
    ToggleOn = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100),
    ToggleOff = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60),
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
    CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12),
    StrokeThickness = 3 -- Increased from 2
}

-- // INSTANCE CREATION HELPER //
local function Create(className, properties, children)
    local instance = Instance.new(className)
    for k, v in pairs(properties or {}) do
        instance[k] = v
    end
    for _, child in pairs(children or {}) do
        child.Parent = instance
    end
    return instance
end

-- // TOXIC LAB BACKGROUND CREATION //
local function CreateToxicLabBackground(parent)
    local labFrame = Create("Frame", {
        Name = "ToxicLabBackground",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 20, 10),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 0,
        Parent = parent
    }, {
        Create("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UI_CONFIG.CornerRadius})
    })
    
    -- Create grid pattern overlay
    local gridOverlay = Create("Frame", {
        Name = "GridOverlay",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = labFrame
    })
    
    -- Vertical grid lines (thicker)
    for i = 0, 10 do
        Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 80, 30),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(i / 10, 0, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), -- Increased from 1 to 2
            ZIndex = 1,
            Parent = gridOverlay
        })
    end
    
    -- Horizontal grid lines (thicker)
    for i = 0, 10 do
        Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 80, 30),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, i / 10, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), -- Increased from 1 to 2
            ZIndex = 1,
            Parent = gridOverlay
        })
    end
    
    -- Toxic bubbles/particles
    for i = 1, 25 do
        local size = math.random(3, 12)
        local bubble = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100),
            BackgroundTransparency = math.random(40, 80) / 100,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(math.random(0, 100) / 100, 0, math.random(0, 100) / 100, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = labFrame
        }, {
            Create("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}),
            Create("UIStroke", {
                Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100),
                Thickness = 2, -- Increased from 1 to 2
                Transparency = 0.5
            })
        })
        
        -- Pulsing glow animation
        task.spawn(function()
            while bubble and bubble.Parent do
                local randomDelay = math.random(10, 30) / 10
                task.wait(randomDelay)
                TweenService:Create(bubble, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = math.random(20, 90) / 100
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
        
        -- Floating animation
        task.spawn(function()
            while bubble and bubble.Parent do
                local randomY = math.random(-15, -5) / 100
                TweenService:Create(bubble, TweenInfo.new(math.random(8, 15), Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                    Position = UDim2.new(
                        bubble.Position.X.Scale + math.random(-5, 5) / 100,
                        0,
                        bubble.Position.Y.Scale + randomY,
                        0
                    )
                }):Play()
                task.wait(math.random(8, 15))
                -- Reset to bottom when reaching top
                if bubble.Position.Y.Scale < -0.1 then
                    bubble.Position = UDim2.new(
                        math.random(0, 100) / 100,
                        0,
                        1.1,
                        0
                    )
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    
    -- Toxic gas clouds
    for i = 1, 4 do
        local gasCloud = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                math.random(0, 50),
                math.random(150, 255),
                math.random(50, 150)
            ),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.88,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(math.random(-20, 100) / 100, 0, math.random(-20, 100) / 100, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, math.random(150, 400), 0, math.random(150, 400)),
            ZIndex = 0,
            Parent = labFrame
        }, {
            Create("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        })
        
        -- Slow drift animation
        task.spawn(function()
            while gasCloud and gasCloud.Parent do
                local randomX = math.random(-15, 15) / 100
                local randomY = math.random(-15, 15) / 100
                TweenService:Create(gasCloud, TweenInfo.new(math.random(20, 30), Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                    Position = UDim2.new(
                        gasCloud.Position.X.Scale + randomX,
                        0,
                        gasCloud.Position.Y.Scale + randomY,
                        0
                    )
                }):Play()
                task.wait(math.random(20, 30))
            end
        end)
    end
    
    -- Hazard symbols (radioactive warning)
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local hazardSymbol = Create("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(math.random(10, 80) / 100, 0, math.random(10, 80) / 100, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            Text = "☢",
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100),
            TextSize = 30,
            TextTransparency = 0.85,
            ZIndex = 1,
            Parent = labFrame
        })
        
        -- Rotate hazard symbol
        task.spawn(function()
            while hazardSymbol and hazardSymbol.Parent do
                TweenService:Create(hazardSymbol, TweenInfo.new(8, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1), {
                    Rotation = 360
                }):Play()
                task.wait(8)
            end
        end)
    end
    
    -- Scan line effect (thicker)
    local scanLine = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.6,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3), -- Increased from 2 to 3
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = labFrame
    })
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while scanLine and scanLine.Parent do
            TweenService:Create(scanLine, TweenInfo.new(3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            }):Play()
            task.wait(3)
            scanLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        end
    end)
    
    return labFrame
end

-- // DRAGGABLE LOGIC - Everything moves together through Container //
local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos

local function setupDrag(frame)
    frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = Container.Position
            
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragInput = input
        end
    end)
end

setupDrag(TitleBox)
setupDrag(Header1)
setupDrag(Header2)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input == dragInput and dragging then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        Container.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)

local LagEnabled = false

local infinityJumpEnabled = false

local performanceEnabled = false
local performanceConnections = {}
local baseTransparencyEnabled = false
local originalTransparency = {}
local unwalkEnabled = false
local unwalkConnections = {}

-- SPOOKY BOOSTER VARIABLES
local spookyBoosterEnabled = false
local spookySpeedValue = 55

local TARGET_GRAVITY = 30
local DEFAULT_GRAVITY = 196.2

-- // SPOOKY BOOSTER LOGIC //
SpookyBoosterToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    spookyBoosterEnabled = not spookyBoosterEnabled
    
    local targetColor = spookyBoosterEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    local buttonText = spookyBoosterEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    TweenService:Create(SpookyBoosterToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { BackgroundColor3 = targetColor }):Play()
    SpookyBoosterToggle.Text = buttonText
end)

SpookySpeedBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if enterPressed then
        local num = tonumber(SpookySpeedBox.Text)
        if num and num > 0 then
            spookySpeedValue = num
        else
            SpookySpeedBox.Text = tostring(spookySpeedValue)
        end
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
        spookyBoosterEnabled = not spookyBoosterEnabled
        
        local targetColor = spookyBoosterEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
        local buttonText = spookyBoosterEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
        
        TweenService:Create(SpookyBoosterToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { BackgroundColor3 = targetColor }):Play()
        SpookyBoosterToggle.Text = buttonText
    end
end)

-- // BASE TRANSPARENCY FUNCTIONALITY //
local function isPlayerBase(obj)
    if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("MeshPart") or obj:IsA("UnionOperation")) then
        return false
    end
    local n = obj.Name:lower()
    local p = obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
    return n:find("base") or n:find("claim") or p:find("base") or p:find("claim")
end

local function enableBaseTransparency()
    for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if isPlayerBase(obj) then
            originalTransparency[obj] = obj.LocalTransparencyModifier
            obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.8
        end
    end
    
    Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if baseTransparencyEnabled and isPlayerBase(obj) then
            originalTransparency[obj] = obj.LocalTransparencyModifier
            obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.8
        end
    end)
end

local function disableBaseTransparency()
    for obj, transparency in pairs(originalTransparency) do
        if obj and obj.Parent then
            obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency
        end
    end
    originalTransparency = {}
end

BaseTransparencyToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    baseTransparencyEnabled = not baseTransparencyEnabled
    BaseTransparencyToggle.BackgroundColor3 = baseTransparencyEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    BaseTransparencyToggle.Text = baseTransparencyEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if baseTransparencyEnabled then
        enableBaseTransparency()
    else
        disableBaseTransparency()
    end
end)

Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    if baseTransparencyEnabled then
        for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if isPlayerBase(obj) then
                originalTransparency[obj] = obj.LocalTransparencyModifier
                obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.8
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- // PERFORMANCE BOOST FUNCTIONALITY //
local function enablePerformance()
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1000
    settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
    Lighting.Brightness = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(160,160,160)
    Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(160,160,160)

    for _, v in Lighting:GetChildren() do
        if v:IsA("PostEffect") then v.Enabled = false end
    end

    local function stripLocal(char)
        if not char then return end
        for _, item in char:GetChildren() do
            if item:IsA("Accessory") or item:IsA("Hat") or item:IsA("Shirt") or
               item:IsA("Pants") or item:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or item:IsA("CharacterMesh") or
               item:IsA("Face") then item:Destroy() end
        end
    end

    if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then stripLocal(Players.LocalPlayer.Character) end
    table.insert(performanceConnections, Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.1) stripLocal(char)
    end))

    local globalStripLoop = task.spawn(function()
        while performanceEnabled do
            for _, model in Workspace:GetChildren() do
                if model:IsA("Model") and model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
                    for _, item in model:GetChildren() do
                        if item:IsA("Accessory") or item:IsA("Hat") or item:IsA("CharacterMesh") or
                           item:IsA("Shirt") or item:IsA("Pants") or item:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or
                           item:IsA("Face") then item:Destroy() end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(performanceConnections, globalStripLoop)

    local flatMaterial = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    local knownClones = {}

    local function flattenAvatar(model)
        if not model then return end
        for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
            if part:IsA("Decal") then part.Texture = "" end
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.Material = flatMaterial
                part.Color = Color3.new(1,1,1)
            end
        end
    end

    local function isCharacter(obj)
        return obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") and obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function onNewObj(obj)
        if isCharacter(obj) and not knownClones[obj] then
            knownClones[obj] = true
            task.spawn(flattenAvatar, obj)
        end
    end

    for _, obj in Workspace:GetChildren() do onNewObj(obj) end
    table.insert(performanceConnections, Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(onNewObj))

    local function applyLowGraphics(v)
        if Players.LocalPlayer.Character and (v:IsDescendantOf(Players.LocalPlayer.Character) or v == Players.LocalPlayer.Character) then return end
        if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
            v.Enabled = false
        end
        if v:IsA("BasePart") then
            v.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            v.Reflectance = 0
        end
        if v:IsA("Texture") or v:IsA("Decal") then v:Destroy() end
    end

    for _, v in Workspace:GetDescendants() do applyLowGraphics(v) end
    table.insert(performanceConnections, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(applyLowGraphics))

    local function disableAnimations(model)
        if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) then return end
        for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
            if v:IsA("AnimationController") or v:IsA("Animator") then v:Destroy()
            elseif v:IsA("Humanoid") then v:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics) end
        end
    end

    local function reduceBrainRot(model)
        if Players.LocalPlayer.Character and (model == Players.LocalPlayer.Character or model:IsDescendantOf(Players.LocalPlayer.Character)) then return end
        if model.Name:lower():find("brainrot") then
            for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") then v.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic v.Reflectance = 0 end
                if v:IsA("AnimationController") or v:IsA("Animator") then v:Destroy() end
                if v:IsA("Texture") or v:IsA("Decal") then v:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end

    local function hideEvents(model)
        if Players.LocalPlayer.Character and (model == Players.LocalPlayer.Character or model:IsDescendantOf(Players.LocalPlayer.Character)) then return end
        local n = model.Name:lower()
        if n:find("fire") or n:find("taco") or n:find("nyan") then
            for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") then v.Transparency = 1 end
                if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then v.Enabled = false end
                if v:IsA("Texture") or v:IsA("Decal") then v:Destroy() end
                if v:IsA("AnimationController") or v:IsA("Animator") then v:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end

    for _, obj in Workspace:GetDescendants() do
        if obj:IsA("Model") then
            disableAnimations(obj)
            reduceBrainRot(obj)
            hideEvents(obj)
        end
    end

    table.insert(performanceConnections, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if desc:IsA("Model") then
            disableAnimations(desc)
            reduceBrainRot(desc)
            hideEvents(desc)
        end
    end))
end

local function disablePerformance()
    for _, c in performanceConnections do
        if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then c:Disconnect()
        elseif typeof(c) == "thread" then task.cancel(c) end
    end
    table.clear(performanceConnections)
end

PerformanceToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    performanceEnabled = not performanceEnabled
    PerformanceToggle.BackgroundColor3 = performanceEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    PerformanceToggle.Text = performanceEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if performanceEnabled then
        enablePerformance()
    else
        disablePerformance()
    end
end)

-- // AUTO GRAB FUNCTIONALITY //
_G.InstaPickup = false

local autoGrabThread = nil

local function startAutoGrab()
    if autoGrabThread then return end
    
    autoGrabThread = task.spawn(function()
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        local function getCharacter()
            return LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        end

        local function getHRP()
            local char = getCharacter()
            return char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        end

        local HRP = getHRP()
        local Humanoid = getCharacter():WaitForChild("Humanoid")

        LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            HRP = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            Humanoid = newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        end)

        local function getPromptPosition(prompt)
            local parent = prompt.Parent

            if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                return parent.Position
            end

            if parent:IsA("Model") then
                local primary = parent.PrimaryPart or parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if primary then
                    return primary.Position
                end
            end

            if parent:IsA("Attachment") then
                return parent.WorldPosition
            end

            local part = parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            return part and part.Position or nil
        end

        local function findNearestStealPrompt()
            local nearest = nil
            local minDist = math.huge

            local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if not plotsFolder then
                return nil, math.huge
            end

            for _, descendant in pairs(plotsFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") 
                   and descendant.Enabled 
                   and descendant.ActionText == "Steal" then
                    
                    local pos = getPromptPosition(descendant)
                    if pos then
                        local distance = (HRP.Position - pos).Magnitude
                        if distance <= descendant.MaxActivationDistance and distance < minDist then
                            minDist = distance
                            nearest = descendant
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return nearest, minDist
        end

        local function triggerPrompt(prompt)
            if not prompt or not prompt:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return end

            pcall(function()
                fireproximityprompt(prompt, 1000, math.huge)
            end)

            pcall(function()
                prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                task.wait(0.03)
                prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
        end

        local currentPrompt = nil
        local currentDistance = math.huge
        local lastScan = 0

        local scanConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not _G.InstaPickup then return end
            
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastScan >= 0.05 then
                currentPrompt, currentDistance = findNearestStealPrompt()
                lastScan = now
            end
        end)

        while _G.InstaPickup do
            task.wait(0.08)

            if not _G.InstaPickup then
                break
            end

            if Humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 25 then
                task.wait(0.1)
                continue
            end

            if currentPrompt and currentDistance <= currentPrompt.MaxActivationDistance then
                triggerPrompt(currentPrompt)
                task.wait(1.30)
            end
        end
        
        if scanConnection then
            scanConnection:Disconnect()
        end
        autoGrabThread = nil
    end)
end

local function stopAutoGrab()
    _G.InstaPickup = false
    if autoGrabThread then
        task.cancel(autoGrabThread)
        autoGrabThread = nil
    end
end

AutoGrabToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    autoGrabEnabled = not autoGrabEnabled
    AutoGrabToggle.BackgroundColor3 = autoGrabEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    AutoGrabToggle.Text = autoGrabEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if autoGrabEnabled then
        _G.InstaPickup = true
        startAutoGrab()
    else
        stopAutoGrab()
    end
end)

-- ESP Functions
local function createESP(plr)
    if plr == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
    if not plr.Character then return end
    if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("followme@rznnq") then return end
    local char = plr.Character
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not (hrp and head) then return end
    
    local hitbox = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
    hitbox.Name = "followme@rznnq"
    hitbox.Adornee = hrp
    hitbox.Size = Vector3.new(4, 6, 2)
    hitbox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
    hitbox.Transparency = 0.6
    hitbox.ZIndex = 10
    hitbox.AlwaysOnTop = true
    hitbox.Parent = char
    
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "cpsito riko"
    billboard.Adornee = head
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.Parent = char
    
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = plr.DisplayName or plr.Name
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Arcade
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.Parent = billboard
end

local function removeESP(plr)
    if not plr.Character then return end
    local hitbox = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("followme@rznnq")
    local nameGui = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("cpsito riko")
    if hitbox then hitbox:Destroy() end
    if nameGui then nameGui:Destroy() end
end

local function enableESP()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            if plr.Character then
                createESP(plr)
            end
            local conn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(0.1)
                if espEnabled then
                    createESP(plr)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(espConnections, conn)
        end
    end
    
    local playerAddedConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
        if plr == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
        
        local charAddedConn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            if espEnabled then
                createESP(plr)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(espConnections, charAddedConn)
    end)
    table.insert(espConnections, playerAddedConn)
end

local function disableESP()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        removeESP(plr)
    end
    for _, conn in ipairs(espConnections) do
        if conn and conn.Connected then
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end
    espConnections = {}
end

-- Infinite Jump Logic
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not infinityJumpEnabled then return end
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and hrp.Velocity.Y < -clampFallSpeed then
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, -clampFallSpeed, hrp.Velocity.Z)
    end
end)

UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if not infinityJumpEnabled then return end
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, jumpForce, hrp.Velocity.Z)
    end
end)

-- // UNWALK (GHOST MODE) FUNCTIONALITY //
local function enableUnwalk()
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    
    local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not humanoid then return end
    
    -- Set movement speeds
    humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
    humanoid.JumpPower = 50
    
    -- Get animation controller
    local animate = char:WaitForChild("Animate", 5)
    if not animate then return end
    
    -- Clear all animations except idle
    for _, v in pairs(animate:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name ~= "idle" then
            if v:IsA("Folder") then
                for _, v2 in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
                    if v2:IsA("StringValue") then
                        v2.Value = ""
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Set idle animation to ghost animation
    local idle = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")
    if idle then
        for _, v in pairs(idle:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("StringValue") then
                v.Value = "rbxassetid://507766388"
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Load ghost animation
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://507766388"
    
    local loadedAnimation = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)
    loadedAnimation.Looped = true
    loadedAnimation.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
    loadedAnimation:AdjustWeight(10)
    loadedAnimation:Play()
    
    -- Keep ghost animation playing
    local heartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not unwalkEnabled then return end
        for _, track in pairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track ~= loadedAnimation then
                track:Stop(0)
            end
        end
        loadedAnimation:AdjustWeight(10)
    end)
    table.insert(unwalkConnections, heartbeatConn)
    
    -- Keep animations cleared
    local clearAnimConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not unwalkEnabled then return end
        if animate.Parent then
            for _, v in pairs(animate:GetChildren()) do
                if v.Name ~= "idle" then
                    if v:IsA("Folder") then
                        for _, v2 in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
                            if v2:IsA("StringValue") then
                                v2.Value = ""
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(unwalkConnections, clearAnimConn)
end

local function disableUnwalk()
    -- Disconnect all connections
    for _, conn in ipairs(unwalkConnections) do
        if conn and conn.Connected then
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end
    unwalkConnections = {}
    
    -- Reset character
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        -- Stop all animations
        for _, track in pairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            track:Stop()
        end
        
        -- Reset speeds
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
        humanoid.JumpPower = 50
    end
end

UnwalkToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    unwalkEnabled = not unwalkEnabled
    UnwalkToggle.BackgroundColor3 = unwalkEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    UnwalkToggle.Text = unwalkEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if unwalkEnabled then
        enableUnwalk()
    else
        disableUnwalk()
    end
end)

-- Anti Ragdoll Functions
local function initAntiRagdoll()
    local ragdollRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Ragdoll"):WaitForChild("Ragdoll")
    local PlayerModule = require(Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("PlayerModule"))
    local Controls = PlayerModule:GetControls()
    local updating = false

    local function getRoot()
        return Players.LocalPlayer.Character and Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function forceEnableControls(duration)
        local start = tick()
        RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if ragdollDisabled or tick() - start > duration then return end
            Controls:Enable()
        end)
    end

    local function addLinearVelocity()
        if ragdollDisabled then return end
        local root = getRoot()
        local humanoid = Players.LocalPlayer.Character and Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not humanoid then return end

        if not root:FindFirstChild("AntiRagdollLV") then
            local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            attachment.Name = "AntiRagdollAttachment"
            attachment.Parent = root
            local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
            lv.Name = "AntiRagdollLV"
            lv.Attachment0 = attachment
            lv.MaxForce = math.huge
            lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
            lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
            lv.Parent = root
        end

        forceEnableControls(0.1)
        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)

        if not updating then
            updating = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while not ragdollDisabled and Players.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime") and root:FindFirstChild("AntiRagdollLV") do
                    local lv = root:FindFirstChild("AntiRagdollLV")
                    if lv and humanoid then
                        local dir = humanoid.MoveDirection
                        if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                            lv.VectorVelocity = dir.Unit * humanoid.WalkSpeed
                        else
                            lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
                updating = false
            end)
        end
    end

    local function removeLinearVelocity()
        local root = getRoot()
        if not root then return end
        local lv = root:FindFirstChild("AntiRagdollLV")
        if lv then lv:Destroy() end
        local att = root:FindFirstChild("AntiRagdollAttachment")
        if att then att:Destroy() end
    end

    Players.LocalPlayer.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
        if ragdollDisabled then return end
        if attributeName == "RagdollEndTime" then
            if Players.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime") then
                addLinearVelocity()
            else
                removeLinearVelocity()
            end
        end
    end)

    ragdollRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(p1, p2)
        if ragdollDisabled then return end
        if p1 == "Destroy" or p2 == "manualD" then
            removeLinearVelocity()
        end
    end)

    local function DisableRagdollFix()
        ragdollDisabled = true
        removeLinearVelocity()
    end

    return DisableRagdollFix
end

AntiRagdollToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local enabled = AntiRagdollToggle.Text == "OFF"
    AntiRagdollToggle.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    AntiRagdollToggle.Text = enabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if enabled then
        ragdollDisabled = false
        disableFunc = initAntiRagdoll()
    else
        if disableFunc then disableFunc() end
        ragdollDisabled = true
        disableFunc = nil
    end
end)

    local enabled = HitboxToggle.Text == "OFF"
    HitboxToggle.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    HitboxToggle.Text = enabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    
    if enabled then
        hitboxConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= Players.LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                    local torso = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or p.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                    if torso then
                        torso.Size = Vector3.new(14, 14, 14)
                        torso.Transparency = 0.6
                        torso.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        if hitboxConn then hitboxConn:Disconnect() hitboxConn = nil end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= Players.LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                local torso = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or p.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                if torso then
                    torso.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                    torso.Transparency = 0
                    torso.CanCollide = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Speed Slider Logic
local draggingSpeedSlider = false
local function UpdateSpeedSlider(input)
    local pos = input.Position
    local relativeX = (pos.X - SpeedSliderBg.AbsolutePosition.X) / SpeedSliderBg.AbsoluteSize.X
    local value = math.clamp(relativeX, 0, 1)
    
    SpeedSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(value, 0, 1, 0)
    
    customSpeed = math.floor(value * 100)
    if customSpeed < 1 then customSpeed = 1 end
    SpeedLabel.Text = string.format("SPEED: %d", customSpeed)
end

SpeedSliderBg.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingSpeedSlider = true
        UpdateSpeedSlider(input)
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if draggingSpeedSlider and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        UpdateSpeedSlider(input)
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingSpeedSlider = false
    end
end)

    jumpEnabled = not jumpEnabled
    JumpToggle.BackgroundColor3 = jumpEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 90)
    JumpToggle.Text = jumpEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
end)

-- Jump Slider Logic
local draggingJumpSlider = false
local function UpdateJumpSlider(input)
    local pos = input.Position
    local relativeX = (pos.X - JumpSliderBg.AbsolutePosition.X) / JumpSliderBg.AbsoluteSize.X
    local value = math.clamp(relativeX, 0, 1)
    
    JumpSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(value, 0, 1, 0)
    
    customJumpPower = math.floor(value * 100 * 10) / 10
    if customJumpPower < 1 then customJumpPower = 1 end
    JumpLabel.Text = string.format("JUMP: %.1f", customJumpPower)
end

JumpSliderBg.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingJumpSlider = true
        UpdateJumpSlider(input)
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if draggingJumpSlider and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        UpdateJumpSlider(input)
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingJumpSlider = false
    end
end)

-- Main Heartbeat Loop
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return end
    
    if spookyBoosterEnabled and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(
            hum.MoveDirection.X * spookySpeedValue,
            hrp.Velocity.Y,
            hum.MoveDirection.Z * spookySpeedValue
        )
    elseif speedEnabled and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
        local currentSpeed = tonumber(customSpeed) or 27
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
            hum.MoveDirection.X * currentSpeed,
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,
            hum.MoveDirection.Z * currentSpeed
        )
    end
    
    if jumpEnabled then
        local currentJump = tonumber(customJumpPower) or 47.5
        
        local existingForce = hrp:FindFirstChild(jumpForceName)
        if not existingForce then
            local bf = Instance.new("BodyForce")
            bf.Name = jumpForceName
            local liftAmount = (DEFAULT_GRAVITY - TARGET_GRAVITY)
            bf.Force = Vector3.new(0, liftAmount * hrp:GetMass(), 0)
            bf.Parent = hrp
        end
        
        hum.UseJumpPower = true
        hum.JumpPower = currentJump
    else
        if hrp:FindFirstChild(jumpForceName) then
            hrp[jumpForceName]:Destroy()
        end
        if hum then
            hum.JumpPower = 50
        end
    end
    
    if spinBotEnabled then
        local spinForce = hrp:FindFirstChild("SpookySpinBot")
        if not spinForce then
            local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
            bav.Name = "SpookySpinBot"
            bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
            bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, spinSpeed, 0)
            bav.Parent = hrp
        end
    else
        if hrp:FindFirstChild("SpookySpinBot") then
            hrp.SpookySpinBot:Destroy()
        end
    end
end)

Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.1)
    if espEnabled then
        enableESP()
    else
        disableESP()
    end
    
    -- Re-enable unwalk if it was enabled
    if unwalkEnabled then
        task.wait(1.5)
        enableUnwalk()
    end
end)
